公司簡介
COMPANY PROFILE
農夫山泉股份有限公司成立於1996年，為 Established in 1996, Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. is a leader in the
中國包裝飲用水及飲料的龍頭企業。我們 packaged drinking water and beverage industry in the PRC. Our
的產品主要覆蓋包裝飲用水、茶飲料、功能 major products include packaged drinking water, tea beverage,
飲料、果汁飲料及農產品等類別。本公司於 functional beverage, juice beverage and agricultural products. The
2001年6月從有限責任公司整體變更為股份 Company was transformed from a limited liability company into a
有限公司，並於2020年9月在香港聯交所主 joint stock company with limited liabilities in June 2001 and listed on
板上市（股份代號：9633.HK）。 the Main Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in September
2020 (stock code: 9633. HK).
我們秉持「天然、健康」的產品理念，堅持使 We believe in offering natural and healthy products and insist on
用優質原料。我們的包裝飲用水全部源自優 using high quality raw materials. All of our packaged drinking water
質天然水源，含天然的礦物元素。我們擁有 products are sourced from quality natural water sources containing
領先的裝備水平、製造能力、強大的供應鏈 natural mineral elements. We possess industry-leading equipment,
管理能力和嚴格的質量保障體系。 manufacturing capabilities, strong supply chain management
capabilities and rigorous quality assurance systems.
我們建立了包裝飲用水和飲料的雙引擎發展 We have established dual growth engines, packaged drinking water
格局，擁有覆蓋各省市及縣級行政區域的全 and beverage and set up a nationwide sales network covering all
國性銷售網絡。我們實行多品牌戰略，具有 provinces, municipalities and prefecture-level administrative divisions
長遠的品牌規劃和強大的品牌傳播能力，鑄 across China. We implement a multi-brand strategy, have long-term
就了中國軟飲料的超級品牌。 visions for our brands and strong brand communication capabilities,
and have built a super brand in the PRC soft beverage market.
2
農夫山泉股份有限公司 NONGFU SPRING CO., LTD. 年報 2024 Annual Report

本公司及創始人的輿論攻擊和惡意詆毀，對 severely impacted in a negative way due to a surge of online attacks

持續。2024年度，本集團錄得包裝飲用水 products. In 2024, revenue from packaged drinking water products
產品收益為人民幣15,952百萬元，較去年同 of the Group was RMB15,952 million, representing a decrease of
期下降21.3%，佔總收益的37.2%。 21.3% as compared with the same period of last year, accounting
for 37.2% of the total revenue.

看清真相。除了發佈官方澄清之外，本集團 every possible means. In addition to official clarification, the Group’s

2024年4月，我們重新推出了純淨水產品。 In April 2024, we relaunched purified drinking water product, with
此舉旨在讓消費者能够更清晰地感知到純 a view to help consumers more clearly distinguish between purified
淨水和天然水的差異。純淨水中不含礦物 water and natural water. Purified water, free of mineral elements,
元素，故口感更佳；天然水中含天然礦物元 offers a cleaner taste, while natural water, enriched with natural
素，因此更適宜長期飲用。我們亦據此推出 minerals, is better suited for long-term consumption. We also
了「綠瓶有點甜，紅瓶更健康」的廣告語。我 introduced the slogan “green bottle is a bit sweet and red bottle is
們的純淨水產品及天然水產品都來自於我們 healthier (綠瓶有點甜，紅瓶更健康)”. Our purified water product is
優質的天然水源。對於純淨水產品，我們僅 made of the same high-quality natural water sources as our natural
推出了550ml一種包裝規格。 water product. For the purified water, we’ve opted to offer only a
single package size of 550ml.

在防治黃龍病研究方面，我們從多項研究結 In our research to combat citrus yellow shoot disease, we
果發現，感染黃龍病的柑橘樹會釋放出揮發 discovered through multiple studies that infected citrus trees
性物質，與健康柑橘樹存在差異，這些物質 release volatile compounds distinct from those of healthy trees.
至今仍無法用儀器完全區分，但狗的鼻子卻 While these compounds remain indistinguishable by instruments,
能嗅到其中的差異，並進行區分。於是，我 dogs can detect the difference. As a result, we innovatively trained
們創新性地培訓工作犬協助搜索病樹。根據 working dogs to assist in identifying diseased trees. Experimental
實驗數據，經過培訓後正式上崗的搜索犬發 data shows that trained detection dogs can identify infected trees
現病樹的準確率可達99%。2023年12月， with an accuracy rate of 99%. In December 2023, in collaboration
中國工作犬管理協會聯合相關單位和農夫 with the China Working Dog Management Association and other
山泉一起，出台了第一個《工作犬搜索柑橘 relevant bodies, Nongfu Spring established the first group standard
黃龍病訓練及使用規範》的團體標準。2024 for Guidelines for Training and Using Working Dogs to Detect Citrus
年，我們的搜索犬已達到14條。 Yellow Shoot Disease. By 2024, our team of detection dogs had
grown to 14.
信息系統建設 Information system buildup
2024年為支持和保障公司的業務戰略發展 In 2024, to bolster and secure the Company’s strategic business
和佈局落地，我們通過賦碼技術給商品建 development and deployment, we employed the coding technology
立獨立身份，實現從工廠、渠道商、終端門 to assign unique identities to each product, achieving comprehensive
店端到端的全鏈路銷售、庫存和貨齡管理， sales, inventory and shelf-life management across the entire supply
提升庫存周轉和貨齡新鮮度，增強品牌競爭 chain, end-to-end from factories to distributors and retail outlets,
力；優化應用架構、技術架構、數據架構， enhancing inventory turnover and ensuring product freshness,
引入AI大語言模型和智能算法建模，實現營 thereby strengthening brand competitiveness. We refined the
銷運營系統高效化、自動化、智能化，提升 application, technical and data architectures, and introduced AI

boosting labor efficiency and productivity and ensuring stringent risk
management in operations.

略發展要求，本著更好的服務客戶的宗旨， by the Company’s strategic development goals and the commitment
在原物料採購、生產到銷售端到端兩化融合 to superior customer service, integrated the artificial intelligence
（數字化與自動化）集成建設的基礎上，引 (AI) technology into the existing digital and automated integration

算力的結合，為業務提供數據驅動的決策支 sourcing to production and sales, offering data-driven support to
持；通過數字化轉型實現生產供應鏈數智化 decision-making by combining algorithms, solvers and computing
的高效運作，從而提升客戶體驗，提高訂單 power. This digital transformation enabled smarter, more efficient

持續經營 GOING CONCERN
根據現行財務預測和可動用的融資，本集團 On the basis of current financial projections and facilities available,
在可見未來有足夠財務資源繼續經營。因此 the Group has adequate financial resources to continue its operation
在編製財務報告時已採用持續經營基準編製。 in the foreseeable future. Accordingly, the financial statements are
prepared on a going concern basis.

有）制定政策，並予以執行； provided by the external audit agency (if any);

reports;

公告，並認為本公司已遵守所有適用的會計 December 31, 2024 and believes that the Company has complied
標準和規定，並做出充分的披露。審計委員 with all applicable accounting standards and regulations and made
會已對本集團2024年風險管理及內部監控 sufficient disclosures. The Audit Committee has reviewed the
工作進行了審核，並認為本公司不存在重大 Group’s risk management and internal control for 2024, finding
風險監控失誤的情形，也未發現重大風險監 that there had been no deficiency in material risk control nor any
控弱項。審計委員會已審閱2024年度審計 weakness in material risk control. The Audit Committee has reviewed
機構費用，並向董事會建議續聘天健會計師 the remuneration of the auditors for 2024 and recommended the

單獨或者合計持有公司百分之十以上股份的 The Company shall convene an extraordinary general meeting at the

但在公司股東大會決議公告之日或前，前述 than 10% of the Company’s shares. However, prior to the date of
股東單獨或者合計持有的公司股份不得低於 announcement of the resolutions approved at the general meeting,
公司有表決權股份總數的百分之十；持股比 the number of the Company’s shares individually or jointly held by the
例不足百分之十時，臨時股東大會就前述股 above shareholders shall not be lower than 10% of the total number
東提出的議案所做出的相關決議無效。 of the Company’s shares with voting rights; should the shareholding
is less than 10%, resolutions passed at the extraordinary general

公司章程修訂 AMENDMENTS TO THE ARTICLES OF ASSOCIATION
於2023年2月，中國國務院及中國證券監督 In February 2023, the State Council of the PRC and the China
管理委員會（「中國證監會」）分別發佈《國務 Securities Regulatory Commission (the “CSRC”) published the
院關於廢止部分行政法規和文件的決定》和 “Decision of the State Council to Repeal Certain Administrative
《境內企業境外發行證券和上市管理試行辦 Regulations and Documents”(《國務院關於廢止部分行政法規
法》（統稱「境內新法規」），自2023年3月31 和文件的決定》) and “Trial Administrative Measures of Overseas
日起生效（「中國法規變動」）。於境內新法規 Securities Offering and Listing by Domestic Companies” (《境內
生效同日，《國務院關於股份有限公司境外 企業境外發行證券和上市管理試行辦法》)(collectively, the “New
募集股份及上市的特別規定》及《到境外上 Domestic Regulations”), respectively, which came into effect on
市公司章程必備條款》（「《必備條款》」）被廢 March 31, 2023 (the “PRC Regulation Changes”). On the same
止，中國發行人應參照中國證監會發佈的 day when the New Domestic Regulations became effective, the
《上市公司章程指引》制定章程。隨後，聯交 “Special Regulations of the State Council on the Overseas Offering
所已就境內新法規的頒佈對《上市規則》作出 and Listing of Shares by Joint Stock Limited Companies”(《國務
相應修訂，其中包括，不再要求中國發行人 院關於股份有限公司境外募集股份及上市的特別規定》) and the
的公司章程加載《必備條款》及其他附帶規定 “Mandatory Provisions for the Articles of Association of Companies
等，自2023年8月1日起生效。 Listed Overseas”《到境外上市公司章程必備條款》(the “Mandatory
Provisions”) were repealed, and PRC issuers shall formulate their
articles of association with reference to the Guidelines for the
Articles of Association of Listed Companies (《上市公司章程指引》)
issued by the CSRC. Subsequently, the Stock Exchange has made
corresponding amendments to the Listing Rules in response to the
promulgation of the New Domestic Regulations, among other things,
the articles of association of PRC issuers are no longer required to
include the Mandatory Provisions and other ancillary requirements,
with effect from August 1, 2023.

督並每年至少檢討一次其運行的有效性。該 risk management and internal control system of the Company,

董事會已對報告期內風險管理和內部監控工 The Board reviewed the risk management and internal control
作進行了檢討，集團截至最後可行日期的風 during the Reporting Period and concluded that there had been no
險管理和內部監控工作開展的結果表明，本 deficiency in material risk control nor any weakness in material risk
集團不存在重大風險監控失誤的情形，也未 control based on the outcome of the risk management and internal
發現重大風險監控弱項。董事會認為本集團 control work implemented by the Group as of the Latest Practicable
的風險管理和內部監控系統是有效及足夠的。 Date. The Board was of the view that the risk management and
internal control system of the Group is effective and sufficient.

舉報政策 WHISTLEBLOWING POLICY
本集團已設立舉報政策和系統，公開發佈了 The Group has established the whistleblowing policy and system,
舉報專用電話和郵箱，並強調對舉報人的 publicly released the special phone number and E-mail address for
保護，對舉報人的個人信息及舉報人提供的 whistleblowing, and emphasized the protection for whistleblowers.
資料均嚴格保密，對惡意打擊報復舉報人員 The personal information of whistleblowers and the information
的行為，將追究相關人員責任。所接獲的 provided by them shall be kept strictly confidential. Any act of
舉報線索，由本集團審計稽查中心獨立開 malicious retaliation against whistleblowers will be held accountable
展第三方調查工作，確保過程和結果客觀 to relevant personnel. The Audit and Inspection Center of the
公正，審計稽查中心定期向審計委員會彙報 Group will independently conduct the third-party investigation on
其工作成果。本集團舉報政策詳情可於本公 the reported clues to ensure the objectivity and impartiality of the
司網站查閱。 process and results. The Audit and Inspection Center will report its
work results to the Audit Committee on a regular basis. Particulars of
the whistleblowing policy of the Group are available for inspection on
the website of the Company.

態度，我們通過《農夫山泉員工商業行為準 tolerance towards bribery and corruption. We have adopted the
則》、《員工獎懲管理辦法》等制度，對員工 “Code of Business Conduct for Employees of Nongfu Spring”, the

勞動合同，納入反舞弊聯盟黑名單等；通 such as fraud, corruption, etc. and the punishment standards, and

股息政策 DIVIDEND POLICY
本公司目前並無任何預先釐定的派息率。董 The Company currently does not have a pre-determined dividend
事會在考慮本集團的經營業績、財務狀況、 payout ratio. The Board may declare and pay dividends after taking
現金流量、營運及資本開支需求、未來業務 into account results of operations, financial condition, cash flow,
發展戰略及預測以及可能認為相關的其他因 operating and capital expenditure requirements, future business
素後宣派及派付股息。本公司的過往股息宣 development strategies and estimates of the Group and other factors
派未必反映日後股息宣派。 as it may deem relevant. Historical declarations of dividends of the
Company may not reflect our future declarations of dividends.
根據中國會計規則及法規，劃撥至法定公積 The allocations to the statutory common reserve are currently
金的金額現時定為有關財政年度本公司權益 determined to be 10% of the Company’s after-tax profit attributable
持有人應佔稅後利潤的10%。當法定公積金 to equity holders of the Company for the fiscal year determined in
累計撥款達本公司註冊資本50%時，本公司 accordance with PRC accounting rules and regulations. When the
毋須再撥款至法定公積金。 accumulated allocations to the statutory common reserve reach 50%
of the registered capital of the Company, it will no longer be required
to make allowances for allocation to the statutory common reserve.

不獲全面豁免持續關連交易的審閱 Review for Non-fully Exempt Continuing Connected
Transactions
根據《上市規則》，鍾先生及養生堂為本公司 Mr. Zhong and Yangshengtang are substantial Shareholders of the
的主要股東，屬本公司的關連人士。因此， Company and the connected persons of the Company under the
上述交易構成本公司的持續關連交易。 Listing Rules. Therefore, the transactions abovementioned constitute
the continuing connected transactions of the Company.

環境政策及表現 ENVIRONMENTAL POLICY AND PERFORMANCE
本集團遵守《中華人民共和國環境保護法》、 The Group complies with relevant environmental protection laws
《中華人民共和國環境影響評價法》、《中華 and regulations, including the Law on Environmental Protection
人民共和國水法》、《建設項目環境保護管理 of the People’s Republic of China, the Environmental Impact
條例》等環境保護相關法律法規。 Assessment Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Water
Law of the People’s Republic of China, and the Regulations on the
Environmental Protection Management of Construction Projects (建
設項目環境保護管理條例) and other laws and regulations.
為了保護水源地環境，與水源地自然生態相 For the protection of the water source environment and the
和諧，本集團盡力減小對周邊環境的影響。 harmonization with the natural habitat of the water source, the Group
並且，本集團對生產及運營中的水資源、包 has made best efforts to minimize the impact on the surrounding
裝材料及能源使用情況、排污及溫室氣體排 environment. In addition, the Group conducts specific monitoring
放情況進行專門監察及管理，嚴格管控生產 and management of water resources, packaging materials and
運營中的環境生態影響。同時，本集團建立 energy usage, sewage and GHG emissions during the production
和不斷完善內部環境保護及可持續發展管理 and operations, and strictly controls the environmental and
制度，通過構建良好的工廠健康、安全與環 ecological impacts during the production and operations. Meanwhile,
境管理體系及秩序，實現對工廠水資源、能 the Group has established and been continuously improving the
源以及三廢排放的全面管理，提升整體環境 internal environmental protection and sustainable development
績效表現。 management system. Through building a good health, safety and
environmental management system and order in the factory, the
overall management of the water resources, energy and three
wastes emissions in the factory will be achieved, and the overall
environmental performance will be improved.

本集團《2024可持續發展報告（ESG報告）》及 please refer to the “2024 Sustainability Report (ESG Report)” and the
《可持續發展公共政策(2023)》。 “Sustainable Development Policy (2023)” of the Group.

本年度的銷售額進行比較；
• 選擇年內銷售獎勵折扣樣本，將各選定項目與相
關銷售合約及客戶協議進行比較，重新計算貴集
團的應付銷售折扣及回扣；及
• 將銷售獎勵折扣及貸項通知的其後實際結算與年
末相應計提金額進行比較。

農夫山泉股份有限公司於2001年6月 Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. was incorporated and registered in the
27日在中華人民共和國（「中國」）註冊 People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) on 27 June 2001. On 8
成立及登記。於2020年9月8日，本公 September 2020, the Company was listed on the Main Board
司於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「香港 of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “HKSE”)
聯交所」）主板上市（股份代號：9633. (stock code: 9633. HK). The address of the registered office
HK）。註冊辦事處地址為浙江省杭州 is No. 181, Geyazhuang, Xihu District, Hangzhou, Zhejiang
市西湖區葛衙莊181號。 Province.
本集團參與以下主要業務： The Group was involved in the following principal activities:
－ 生產及銷售包裝飲用水及飲料 – Production and sale of packaged water and beverage
－ 銷售農產品 – Sale of agricultural products

終控股公司為養生堂有限公司，該公 ultimate holding company of the Company is Yangshengtang
司於中國註冊成立。 Co., Ltd., which is incorporated and registered in the PRC.

2.1 編製基準 2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION
該等綜合財務報表乃根據國際財務報 These consolidated financial statements have been prepared in
告會計準則編製，當中包括國際會計 accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards, which comprise
準則理事會（「國際會計準則理事會」） standards and interpretations approved by the International
批准的準則及詮釋，及國際會計準則 Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”), and International
理事會批准的國際會計準則及常務詮 Accounting Standards and Standing Interpretations Committee
釋委員會詮釋以及香港公司條例之披 interpretations approved by the International Accounting
露規定。 Standards Board and the disclosure requirements of the Hong
Kong Companies Ordinance.
綜合財務報表乃按照歷史成本原則編 The consolidated financial statements have been prepared
製。除有特別註明外，該等財務報表 under the historical cost convention. These financial statements
以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，且所有數 are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded
值均約整至最近的千元單位。 to the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.
於2024年12月31日，本集團錄得流動 The Group recorded net current liabilities of RMB912,863,000
負債淨額人民幣912,863,000元。鑒於 as at 31 December 2024. In view of the net current liabilities
流動負債淨額狀況，董事在評估本集 position, the directors have given careful consideration to the
團是否有充足財務資源持續經營時， future liquidity and performance of the Group and its available
已仔細考慮本集團未來流動資金及表 sources of finance in assessing whether the Group will have
現以及其可用的資金來源。 sufficient financial resources to continue as a going concern.

自由兌換為現金，董事確信本集團可 RMB10,630,882,000, are freely convertible into cash, the
在可見未來全額履行到期的財務義務。 directors are satisfied that the Group is able to meet in full its

公平值計量 Fair value measurement
本集團計量各報告期末的衍生金融工 The Group measures its derivative financial instruments and
具及股本投資。公平值為市場參與者 equity investments at the end of each reporting period. Fair
於計量日期在有序交易中出售資產所 value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
收取或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公平 paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
值計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉讓負 market participants at the measurement date. The fair value
債的交易於資產或負債的主要市場或 measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction
（在無主要市場的情況下）資產或負債 to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the
的最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市 principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a
場必須為本集團可進入的市場。資產 principal market, in the most advantageous market for the asset
或負債的公平值乃採用市場參與者為 or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market
資產或負債定價時所用的假設計量， must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset
並假設市場參與者依照彼等的最佳經 or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market
濟利益行事。 participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
assuming that market participants act in their economic best
interest.
非金融資產的公平值計量經計及市場 A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into
參與者能否以最高及最佳用途使用有 account a market participant’s ability to generate economic
關資產或能否將有關資產出售予會以 benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by
最高及最佳用途使用有關資產的另一 selling it to another market participant that would use the asset
名市場參與者以產生經濟利益。 in its highest and best use.
本集團採取適用於不同情況且具備充 The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in
分數據以供計量公平值的估值技巧， the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to
以盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡 measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable
量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。 inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

產生的資產及負債賬面值之任何公平 and any fair value adjustments to the carrying amounts of assets
值調整，均視作海外業務的資產及負 and liabilities arising on acquisition are treated as assets and
債處理，並按收市匯率換算。 liabilities of the foreign operation and translated at the closing
rate.
就綜合現金流量表而言，海外附屬公 For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash flows,
司的現金流量按現金流量產生日期的 the cash flows of overseas subsidiaries are translated into
通行匯率換算為人民幣。海外附屬公 RMB at the exchange rates ruling at the dates of the cash
司於整個年度內經常產生的現金流量 flows. Frequently recurring cash flows of overseas subsidiaries
乃按年內的加權平均匯率換算為人民 which arise throughout the year are translated into RMB at the
幣。 weighted average exchange rates for the year.

不確定性可導致需要對未來受影響的 assumptions and estimates could result in outcomes that

銷售獎勵折扣的可變代價 Variable consideration for volume rebates
本集團估計將計入附有銷售獎勵折扣 The Group estimates variable consideration to be included
的產品銷售交易價格的可變代價。 in the transaction price for the sales of products with volume
rebates.
本集團就設有單一數量限額的合約按 The Group’s volume rebates are analysed on a per customer
每名客戶作基準分析銷售獎勵折扣。 basis for contracts that are subject to a single volume
釐定客戶是否可能有權獲得折扣取決 threshold. Determining whether a customer is likely to be
於客戶過往享有的折扣及迄今的累計 entitled to a rebate depends on the customer’s historical
採購量。 rebate entitlement and accumulated purchases to date.
本集團每年更新其對預期銷售獎勵折 The Group updates its assessment of expected volume
扣的評估，並相應調整應付銷售折扣 rebates yearly and the sales discount and rebate payables are
及獎勵折扣。預期獎勵折扣的估計對 adjusted accordingly. Estimates of expected volume rebates
情況變動敏感，而本集團有關獎勵折 are sensitive to changes in circumstances and the Group’s
扣配額的過往經驗未必能代表客戶日 past experience regarding rebate entitlements may not be
後的實際獎勵折扣配額。 representative of a customer’s actual rebate entitlements in the
future.

飲料產品分部；及 manufacture and sale of juice beverage products; and

其他產品分部。 and sale of agricultural products and other beverages.

現。分部表現乃基於經調整除稅前溢 resource allocation and performance assessment. Segment
利而計量之可呈報分部溢利作出評 performance is evaluated based on reportable segment profit,
估。除利息收入、財務費用、其他收 which is a measure of adjusted profit before tax. The adjusted
入及收益以及總部和企業開支於該計 profit before tax is measured consistently with the Group’s

由於管理層並非定期審閱該等資料以 expenses are excluded from such measurement. No analysis
作資源分配及表現評估，故並無呈列 of segment assets and liabilities is presented as management

本集團採用簡化法計提國際財務報告 The Group applies the simplified approach to providing for
準則第9號所規定的預期信貸虧損，其 expected credit losses prescribed by IFRS 9, which permits the
允許就所有貿易應收款項使用全期預 use of the lifetime expected credit loss provision for all trade
期信貸虧損撥備。 receivables.
應收票據為獲銀行於期限內無條件接 Bills receivable are bank acceptance bills that are
納的銀行承兌票據，且並不會就應收 unconditionally accepted by banks within the maturity period,
票據減值計提虧損撥備。 and there is no loss allowance for impairment of bills receivable.
年末使用撥備矩陣進行減值分析，以 An impairment analysis is performed at year end using a
計量貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損。 provision matrix to measure expected credit losses for trade
撥備率乃基於具有類似虧損模式的多 receivables. The provision rates are based on days past due
個客戶分部組別的逾期日數釐定。該 for groupings of various customer segments with similar loss
計算反映了概率加權結果，貨幣時間 patterns. The calculation reflects the probability-weighted
價值以及年末可獲得的關於過往事 outcome, the time value of money and reasonable and
件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀況預測的 supportable information that is available at year end about past
合理及可支持的資料。 events, current conditions and forecasts of future economic
conditions.
當有資訊表明交易對手處於嚴重財務 The Group writes off trade receivables when there is
困難且並無實際收回可能，本集團將 information indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial
核銷其貿易應收款項。本集團亦在合 difficulties and there is no realistic prospect of recovery. The
適時考慮法律意見，例如交易對手已 Group also takes into account legal advice where appropriate,
被清盤或已進入破產程序，以較早者 e.g. when the counterparty has been placed under liquidation
為準。 or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, whichever occurs
sooner.

31. 儲備 31. RESERVES
本集團的儲備金額及其變動於財務報 The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements
表的綜合權益變動表呈列。 therein are presented in the consolidated statement of changes
in equity in the financial statements.
(a) 法定儲備 (a) Statutory reserve
根據中華人民共和國公司法，中 In accordance with the Company Law of the People’s
國公司須將法定除稅後利潤的 Republic of China, the companies in the PRC are
10%轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲備 required to allocate 10% of the statutory after tax profits
的累計總額達到公司註冊資本的 to the statutory reserve until the cumulative total of
50%。待中國有關部門批准後， the reserve reaches 50% of the companies’ registered
法定儲備可用於抵銷任何累計虧 capital. Subject to the approval of the relevant PRC
損或增加公司的註冊資本。法定 authorities, the statutory reserve may be used to offset
儲備不可用於向中國附屬公司股 any accumulated losses or increase the registered capital
東分派股息。 of the companies. The statutory reserve is not available
for dividend distribution to shareholders of the PRC
subsidiaries.
(b) 資本儲備 (b) Capital reserve
本集團的資本儲備指最終控股公 The capital reserve of the Group represents the
司及股東的貢獻及代價超出所收 contribution from the ultimate holding company and
購非控股權益的賬面值的部分。 shareholders and the excess of the consideration over
the carrying amount of the non-controlling interests
acquired.
(c) 匯兌波動儲備 (c) Exchange fluctuation reserve
匯兌波動儲備指因換算其功能貨 The exchange fluctuation reserve represents exchange
幣與本集團呈列貨幣不同的海外 differences arising from the translation of the financial
業務的財務報表而產生的匯兌差 statements of foreign operations whose functional
額。 currencies are different from the Group’s presentation
currency.
(d) 股份支付儲備 (d) Share-based payment reserve
股份支付儲備包括尚未歸屬之股 The share-based payment reserve comprises the fair
份獎勵公平值，詳見財務報表附 value of shares awarded which are yet to be vested, as
註2.4有關股份支付之會計政策。 further explained in the accounting policy for share-based
payments in note 2.4 to the financial statements.

資本管理 Capital management
本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本 The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are
集團持續經營的能力以及維持穩健的 to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern
資本比率，以支持其業務及為股東爭 and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its
取最大價值。 business and maximise shareholders’ value.
本集團考慮經濟狀況變化及相關資產 The Group manages its capital structure and makes
的風險特徵來管理其資本架構並作出 adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions
相應調整。為維持或調整資本架構， and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To
本集團或會調整支付予股東的股息、 maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust
退回股本予股東或發行新股。本集團 the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital to
不受任何外部資本限制的約束。年 shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not subject to
內，本集團的資本管理目標、政策或 any externally imposed capital requirements. No changes were
程序未發生變化。 made in the objectives, policies or processes for managing
capital during the year.
本集團採用資本負債比率監控資本， The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is debt
該比率按債務除以經調整資本計算。 divided by the adjusted capital. Debt includes interest-bearing
債務包括計息借貸以及租賃負債。資 borrowings and lease liabilities. Capital represents equity
本指母公司擁有人應佔權益。於報告 attributable to owners of the parent. The gearing ratios as at
期末的資本負債比率如下： the end of the reporting periods were as follows:

的公平值按具有類似條款、信貸風險 portion of lease liabilities have been calculated by discounting
及剩餘到期日的工具的現行利率折現 the expected future cash flows using rates currently available

本集團的企業融資團隊負責制定金融 The Group’s corporate finance team is responsible for
工具公平值管理的政策及程序。企業 determining the policies and procedures for the fair value
融資團隊直接向財務總監及董事會匯 management of financial instruments. The corporate finance
報。於各報告期，企業融資團隊會分 team reports directly to the chief financial officer and the board
析金融工具價值的變動及釐定估值所 of directors. At each reporting date, the corporate finance team
用的主要輸入數據。財務總監已審閱 analyses the movements in the values of financial instruments
並批准估值，且就年度財務報告與董 and determines the major inputs applied in the valuation.
事會討論估值過程及結果。 The valuation is reviewed and approved by the chief financial
officer. The valuation process and results are discussed with
the board of directors for annual financial reporting.
金融資產及負債的公平值按當前交易 The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included
（強制或清算出售除外）中雙方自願進 at the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged
行工具交換的金額入賬。以下為用於 in a current transaction between willing parties, other than
估計公平值的方法及假設。 in a forced or liquidation sale. The following methods and
assumptions were used to estimate the fair values.
本集團投資於非上市投資，即中國內 The Group invests in unlisted investments, which represent
地銀行發行的理財產品。本集團採用 wealth management products issued by banks in Mainland
折現現金流量估值模式，根據具類似 China. The Group has estimated the fair value of these unlisted
條款及風險的工具的市場利率，估計 investments by using a discounted cash flow valuation model
該等非上市投資的公允價值。 based on the market interest rates of instruments with similar
terms and risks.
